     million for the year ended December 31, 2021 (2020:                實 現 綜 合 營 業 額 人 民 幣44.47億 元（2020年

     revenue was mainly attributable to the improved operating          21.15億元）；營業額的相關增長一方面是因
     efficiency as well as the effects in relation to the acquisition   為經營效益改善，另一方面亦因完成多家醫
     of Huaiyin Hospital in June 2021 and the consolidation of          院的併表安排以及於2021年6月份完成收購
     certain hospitals (for details, please refer to the section        淮陰醫院而產生（詳情見下文「併表一系列醫
     headed “Consolidating certain hospitals” below).                   院」章節）。

     During the Reporting Period, the Group recorded a net profit       而報告期內本集團錄得淨利潤為人民幣4.26
     of RMB426 million (2020: RMB320 million; 2019: RMB401              億 元（2020年 度：人 民 幣3.20億 元；2019年

     per share; 2019: RMB0.31 per share). The increase in net           元（2020年 度：每 股 人 民 幣0.25元；2019年

     strengthening the management of our member hospitals’              成員醫院的日常運營管理、推動提升服務品

     new normal with the epidemic and improved their overall

Future Outlook                                                   未來展望
The medical industry in China is generally large in scale with   我國醫療行業整體規模大、發展潛力高，患
high development potential. As patients crave for more           者就醫追求更先進的醫療技術、更好的就醫
advanced medical technology and better consultation              體驗，而醫院必須提供「更專業、更有效、更
experience, hospitals need to provide “more professional,        經濟」的醫療服務。華潤集團對醫療行業的
more effective and more economical” medical services. The        長期看好，並對華潤醫療提供堅定的支援。
China Resources Group has been providing strong support to       十 四 五 開 局 之 年，華 潤 醫 療 經 過 不 斷 的 研

losses or liabilities incurred or sustained by him as a          刑事法律訴訟中進行抗辯而招致或蒙受的一
Director, in defending any proceedings, whether civil or         切損失或責任。
criminal, in which judgment is given in his favor, or in which
he is acquitted.

     Practices and Conduct of Meetings                               會議常規和操守
     Notice of regular Board meetings had been served to all         常規董事會會議通告已於會前最少14日送達
     Directors at least 14 days prior to the meetings to give all    全體董事，以讓全體董事均有機會出席。
     Directors an opportunity to attend.

     For regular Board and committee meetings, all agendas,          就 常 規 董 事 會 及 委 員 會 會 議 而 言，所 有 議
     meeting papers, together with all applicable, complete and      程、會議文件連同一切適用、完整及可靠之
     reliable statistics will be sent to all the Directors or        數據將於會前至少三天發送全體董事或委員
     committees at least three days before a meeting is held. All    會。全體董事均可提出任何事項列入董事會
     Directors may propose any business to be included in the        及委員會會議議程，並可與公司秘書聯絡以
     agenda of the Board or committee meetings and contact the       確保全部董事會程序及適用規章均獲全面遵
     company secretary to ensure full compliance with all of the     守。
     Board’s procedures and applicable regulations.

     the regular Board meetings and, if necessary, other Board       董事會會議，並於必要時出席其他董事會及
     and committee meetings to advise on the Group’s business        委員會會議，以就本集團業務發展、財務及
     development, financial and accounting matters, statutory and    會計事宜、法定及監管合宜、企業管治及風
     regulatory compliance, corporate governance and risk            險管理以及其他要事提供意見。董事會可於
     management as well as other significant issues. The Board       恰當時授權董事尋求獨立專業意見，費用一
     may, if appropriate, authorize the Directors to seek            概由本集團承擔。董事會秘書將詳盡記錄董
     independent and professional advice at the expense of the       事會會議上所討論及議決的事宜，並須撰寫
     Group. Matters discussed and resolved at Board meetings         會議記錄或決議案及將其存檔。一般而言，
     will be recorded in detail by the Board secretary who shall     董事會會議記錄的草擬本應於會後一段合理
     prepare and file the minutes or resolutions. Generally, draft   時間內發予董事，以供彼等給予意見，而定

Auditor’s responsibilities for the audit of                      核數師就審計綜合財務報表承擔
the consolidated financial statements                            的責任
Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about          我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不
whether the consolidated financial statements as a whole         存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述
are free from material misstatement, whether due to fraud        取得合理保證，並出具包括我們意見的核數
or error, and to issue an auditor’s report that includes our     師報告。我們僅對全體股東作出報告，除此
opinion. Our report is made solely to you, as a body, and for    以外，本報告並無其他用途。我們不會就核
no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility towards        數師報告的內容向任何其他人士負上或承擔
or accept liability to any other person for the contents of      任何責任。
this report.

Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not 合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證按照
a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with 《香港審計準則》進行的審計，在某一重大錯
HKSAs will always detect a material misstatement when it 誤陳述存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺
exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are 詐或錯誤引起，如果合理預期它們單獨或匯
considered material if, individually or in the aggregate, they 總起來可能影響綜合財務報表使用者依賴綜
could reasonably be expected to influence the economic 合財務報表所作出的經濟決定，則有關的錯
decisions of users taken on the basis of these consolidated 誤陳述可被視作重大。
financial statements.

As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise        在根據《香港審計準則》進行審計的過程中，
professional judgement and maintain professional scepticism      我 們 運 用 了 專 業 判 斷，保 持 了 專 業 懷 疑 態
throughout the audit. We also:                                   度。我們亦：

•    Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement      •   識別和評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致綜合
     of the consolidated financial statements, whether due           財務報表存在重大錯誤陳述的風險，設
     to fraud or error, design and perform audit procedures          計及執行審計程序以應對這些風險，以
     responsive to those risks, and obtain audit evidence            及獲取充足和適當的審計憑證，作為我
     that is sufficient and appropriate to provide a basis for       們 意 見 的 基 礎。由 於 欺 詐 可 能 涉 及 串
     our opinion. The risk of not detecting a material               謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛假陳述，或凌
     misstatement resulting from fraud is higher than for            駕於內部控制之上，因此未能發現因欺
     one resulting from error, as fraud may involve                  詐而導致的重大錯誤陳述的風險高於未
     collusion, forgery, intentional omissions,                      能發現因錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述的
     misrepresentations, or the override of internal control.        風險。

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT                                    2.4 主要會計政策概要
    ACCOUNTING POLICIES
  Investments in associates and joint                           於聯營公司及合營公司之投資
  ventures
  An associate is an entity in which the Group has a            聯營公司為本集團於其一般不少於20%
  long term interest of generally not less than 20% of          股本投票權中擁有長期權益的實體，且
  the equity voting rights and over which it is in a            可對其發揮重大影響力。重大影響力指
  position to exercise significant influence. Significant       的是參與投資對象的財務和經營決策的
  influence is the power to participate in the financial        權力，但不是控制或共同控制這些決策
  and operating policy decisions of the investee, but is        的權力。
  not control or joint control over those policies.

  A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement whereby        合營公司指一類共同安排，對安排擁有
  the parties that have joint control of the arrangement        共同控制權之訂約方據此有權享有合營
  have rights to the net assets of the joint venture. Joint     公司之資產淨值。共同控制指對某項安
  control is the contractually agreed sharing of control of     排之訂約協定共同控制，共同控制僅於
  an arrangement, which exists only when decisions              相關活動之決定需共享控制權之各方一
  about the relevant activities require the unanimous           致同意之時存在。
  consent of the parties sharing control.

  The Group’s investments in associates and a joint             本集團於聯營公司及合營公司的投資乃
  venture are stated in the consolidated statement of           按本集團根據權益會計法應佔資產淨值
  financial position at the Group’s share of net assets         減任何減值虧損於綜合財務狀況報表列
  under the equity method of accounting, less any               賬。倘會計政策存在任何不一致，將會
  impairment losses. Adjustments are made to bring into         作出相應調整。本集團應佔聯營公司及
  line any dissimilar accounting policies that may exist.       合營公司收購後業績及其他全面收入分
  The Group’s share of the post-acquisition results and         別計入綜合損益報表及綜合其他全面收
  other comprehensive income of associates and a joint          入。此外，倘於聯營公司或合營公司的
  venture is included in the consolidated statement of          權益直接確認變動，則本集團會於綜合
  profit or loss and consolidated other comprehensive           權益變動報表確認其應佔任何變動（倘
  income, respectively. In addition, when there has been        適用）。本集團與其聯營公司或合營公
  a change recognised directly in the equity of the             司間交易的未變現收益及虧損將以本集
  associate or joint venture, the Group recognises its          團於聯營公司或合營公司的投資為限對
  share of any changes, when applicable, in the                 銷，惟倘未變現虧損證明所轉讓資產減
  consolidated statement of changes in equity.                  值則除外。收購聯營公司或合營公司所
  Unrealised gains and losses resulting from transactions       產生的商譽已計入作本集團於聯營公司
  between the Group and its associates or joint venture         或合營公司投資的一部分。
  are eliminated to the extent of the Group’s
  investments in the associates or joint venture, except
  where unrealised losses provide evidence of an
  impairment of the assets transferred. Goodwill arising
  from the acquisition of associates or joint venture is
  included as part of the Group’s investments in
  associates or joint venture.

           (continued)

           After initial recognition, goodwill is measured at cost         初步確認後，商譽按成本減任何累計減
           less any accumulated impairment losses. Goodwill is             值虧損計量。如有任何事件或情況變動
           tested for impairment annually or more frequently if            表明賬面值可能減值，將每年或更頻繁

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT                                      2.4 主要會計政策概要（續）
    ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)
   Fair value measurement                                         公允價值計量
   The Group measures its financial assets at FVTPL at            本集團於各報告期末按公允價值計量以
   fair value at the end of each reporting period. Fair           公允價值計量且其變動計入損益的金融
   value is the price that would be received to sell an           資產。公允價值乃在市場參與者於計量
   asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly            日期進行的有序交易中出售資產所收取
   transaction between market participants at the                 或轉移負債所支付的價格。公允價值計
   measurement date. The fair value measurement is                量乃基於假設出售資產或轉移負債的交
   based on the presumption that the transaction to sell          易於資產或負債的主要市場進行或於未
   the asset or transfer the liability takes place either in      有主要市場的情況下，則於資產或負債
   the principal market for the asset or liability, or in the     的最有利市場進行。主要或最有利市場
   absence of a principal market, in the most                     須位於本集團能到達的地方。資產或負
   advantageous market for the asset or liability. The            債的公允價值乃基於市場參與者為資產
   principal or the most advantageous market must be              或負債定價所用的假設計量（假設市場
   accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or         參與者依照彼等的最佳經濟利益行事）
                                                                                  。
   a liability is measured using the assumptions that
   market participants would use when pricing the asset
   or liability, assuming that market participants act in
   their economic best interest.

   The Group uses valuation techniques that are                   本集團使用適用於不同情況的估值方
   appropriate in the circumstances and for which                 法，而其有足夠資料計量公允價值，以
   sufficient data are available to measure fair value,           盡量使用相關可觀察輸入數據及盡量減
   maximising the use of relevant observable inputs and           少使用不可觀察輸入數據。
   minimising the use of unobservable inputs.

           case of a financial asset not at fair value through profit        已應用可行權宜方法的應收貿易款項按
           or loss, transaction costs. Trade receivables that do not         國際財務報告準則第15號釐定的交易

           measured at the transaction price determined under

           “Revenue recognition” below.

           In order for a financial asset to be classified and               金融資產需產生純粹為支付本金及未償
           measured at amortised cost, it needs to give rise to              還本金利息（「純粹為支付本金及利息」）
           cash flows that are solely payments of principal and              的現金流量，方可按攤銷成本進行分類
           interest (“SPPI”) on the principal amount outstanding.            及計量。現金流量不是純粹為支付本金
           Financial assets with cash flows that are not SPPI are            及利息的金融資產被分類為以公允價值

           loss, irrespective of the business model.                         業務模式無關。

           order to generate cash flows. The business model                  務模式確定現金流量是否來自收集合約
           determines whether cash flows will result from                    現 金 流 量、出 售 金 融 資 產，或 兩 者 兼
           collecting contractual cash flows, selling the financial          有。以攤銷成本分類和計量的金融資產

           measured at amortised cost are held within a business             金融資產以收集合同現金流量。不屬於

        without material delay to a third party under a               已收取現金流量悉數支付第三
        “pass-through” arrangement; and either (a) the                方；且(a)本集團已轉讓該資產的
        Group has transferred substantially all the risks             絕大部分風險及回報或(b)本集團
        and rewards of the asset, or (b) the Group has                並無轉讓或保留該資產的絕大部
        neither transferred nor retained substantially all            分風險及回報，惟已轉讓該資產
        the risks and rewards of the asset, but has                   之控制權。
        transferred control of the asset.

   When the Group has transferred its rights to receive          倘本集團已轉讓自資產收取現金流量之
   cash flows from an asset or has entered into a                權利或訂立轉付安排，其將評估是否及
   pass-through arrangement, it evaluates if, and to what        於何等程度保留資產所有權的風險及回
   extent, it has retained the risk and rewards of               報。當其並無轉讓或保留資產的絕大部
   ownership of the asset. When it has neither                   分風險及回報，亦無轉讓該資產之控制
   transferred nor retained substantially all the risks and      權，則該資產將按本集團持續涉及有關
   rewards of the asset nor transferred control of the           資 產 的 程 度 確 認 轉 讓 資 產。於 該 情 況
   asset, the Group continues to recognise the                   下，本集團亦確認一項關連負債。已轉
   transferred asset to the extent of the Group’s                讓資產及關連負債乃按反映本集團已保
   continuing involvement. In that case, the Group also          留權利及責任的基準計量。
   recognises an associated liability. The transferred asset
   and the associated liability are measured on a basis
   that reflects the rights and obligations that the Group
   has retained.

   Continuing involvement that takes the form of a               若以就已轉讓資產作出保證的形式持續
   guarantee over the transferred asset is measured at           涉及，則按該項資產之原賬面值與本集

      2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT                                         2.4 主要會計政策概要（續）
          ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)
           Impairment of financial assets                                    金融資產減值
           The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit             本集團就並非以公允價值計量且其變動
           losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair         計入損益的所有債務工具確認預期信貸
           value through profit or loss. ECLs are based on the               虧 損（「預 期 信 貸 虧 損」）的 撥 備。預 期
           difference between the contractual cash flows due in              信貸虧損乃基於根據合約到期的合約現
           accordance with the contract and all the cash flows               金流量與本集團預期收取的所有現金流
           that the Group expects to receive, discounted at an               量之間的差額而釐定，並以原實際利率
           approximation of the original effective interest rate. The        的近似值貼現。預期現金流量將包括出
           expected cash flows will include cash flows from the              售所持抵押品或組成合約條款的其他信
           sale of collateral held or other credit enhancements              貸提升措施的現金流量。
           that are integral to the contractual terms.

           General approach                                                  一般方法
           ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit                     預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就自首
           exposures for which there has not been a significant              次確認起未有顯著增加的信貸風險而
           increase in credit risk since initial recognition, ECLs are       言，須 為 未 來12個 月 內 可 能 發 生 違 約
           provided for credit losses that result from default               事件而導致的信貸虧損（12個月預期信
           events that are possible within the next 12 months (a             貸虧損）計提預期信貸虧損。就自初始
           12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for which               確認起經已顯著增加的信貸風險而言，
           there has been a significant increase in credit risk              不論何時發生違約，於餘下風險年期內
           since initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for       的預期信貸虧損均須計提虧損撥備（全
           credit losses expected over the remaining life of the             期預期信貸虧損）。
           exposure, irrespective of the timing of the default (a
           lifetime ECL).

           At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether                於各報告日期，本集團評估金融工具的
           the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased           信貸風險自首次確認以來是否大幅增
           significantly since initial recognition. When making the          加。進行評估時，本集團會比較報告日
           assessment, the Group compares the risk of a default              期就金融工具產生的違約風險與於首次
           occurring on the financial instrument as at the                   確認日期就金融工具產生的違約風險，
           reporting date with the risk of a default occurring on            並會考慮合理及有理據且毋須花費過度
           the financial instrument as at the date of initial                成本或精力即可獲得的資料，包括歷史
           recognition and considers reasonable and supportable              及前瞻性資料。
           information that is available without undue cost or
           effort, including historical and forward-looking
           information.

                 foreseeable future and future taxable profit will be              會確認遞延稅項資產。
                 available against which the temporary differences
                 can be utilised.

           The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed             遞延稅項資產的賬面值乃於各報告期間
           at the end of each reporting period and reduced to the             結算日進行審閱，並予以扣減，直至不
           extent that it is no longer probable that sufficient               再可能有足夠應課稅利潤以致可動用全
           taxable profit will be available to allow all or part of the       部或部分遞延稅項資產為止。未確認的
           deferred tax asset to be utilised. Unrecognised                    遞延稅項資產乃按可能獲得足夠應課稅
           deferred tax assets are reassessed at the end of each              利潤以致可收回全部或部分遞延稅項資

           would be reflected in a separate financing transaction          過一年的重大財務利益的融資部分時，

           Impairment testing of goodwill and other                       商譽及無限可使用年期的其他

           lives (continued)
           The recoverable amounts of the Ample Mighty CGUs,              廣雄現金產生單位、重汽現金產生單位
           the Zhong Qi CGU and the Sinophi Group CGU have                及Sinophi集團現金產生單位之可收回
           been determined based on fair value less costs of              金額乃根據其公允價值減去處置成本後
           disposal calculations using cash flow projections based        而確定的，使用的現金流量預測乃基於
           on financial budgets covering a five-year period               高級管理層已審批的涵蓋五年期間的財
           approved by senior management and are within Level             務預算而推算，此可收回金額為公允價
           3 fair value measurement. The discount rate applied to         值計量的第三級。應用於現金流量預測
           the cash flow projections is 15% (2020: 15%). The              的 貼 現 率 為15%（2020年：15%）。用
           growth rate used to extrapolate the cash flows of the          於推斷（超過五年期）廣雄現金產生單
           Ample Mighty CGUs, the Zhong Qi CGU and the                    位、重 汽 現 金 產 生 單 位 及Sinophi集 團
           Sinophi Group CGU beyond the five-year period is 3%            現金產生單位之現金流量的增長率為
           (2020: 3%). This growth rate does not exceed the long          3%（2020年：3%）。該 增 長 率 不 超 過
           term average growth rates for the relevant markets.            相關市場上的長期平均增長率。

           Assumptions were used in the fair value less costs of          有關假設已在計算於2021年12月31日

21. RECEIVABLES FROM IOT HOSPITALS                           21. 來自IOT醫院之應收款項（續）
   (continued)

   Pursuant to the IOT arrangements with certain not-for-      根據與若干非營利醫院的IOT安排，本
   profit hospitals, the Group made investment amounts         集團向IOT醫院作出投資金額，該等金
   to the IOT hospitals that will be repaid to the Group       額 將 償 還 給 本 集 團（「應 償 還 投 資 金
   (the “Repayable Investment Amounts”) in return for          額」），以換取IOT醫院的經營權，為期
   the operating rights of the IOT hospitals over a tenure     16 至 30 年。應 償 還 投 資 金 額 為 無 抵
   ranging from 16 to 30 years. The Repayable                  押、免息，並將於IOT安排期間按等額
   Investment Amounts are unsecured, interest-free and         年度分期償還予本集團。
   will be repaid to the Group in equal annual instalments

   The sensitivity analysis above has been determined          上文呈列的敏感度分析是根據一種方法
   based on a method that extrapolates the impact on           而得出，該方法可推斷由於在報告期末

   changes in key assumptions occurring at the end of          任產生的影響。敏感度分析基於重要假
   the reporting period. The sensitivity analysis is based     設 的 變 化，而 所 有 其 他 假 設 則 維 持 不
   on a change in a significant assumption, keeping all        變。上文呈列的敏感度分析未必反映界
   other assumptions constant. The sensitivity analysis        定福利責任的實際變化，因為假設不大

   defined benefit obligations as it is unlikely that

39. SHARE AWARD SCHEME                                       39. 股份獎勵計劃
  On 7 July 2014, the Company adopted the Share                於2014年7月7日，本公司採納股份獎
  Award Scheme as a means to recognise the                     勵計劃，以表彰主要管理人員、聘用專

  management personnel, employed experts and core
  employees.

  include the Group’s directors, senior management,            的董事、本集團高級管理層、受聘的專
  employed experts and core employees of the Group.            家 和 核 心 員 工。股 份 獎 勵 計 劃 自2014
  The Share Award Scheme will remain in force for 10           年7月7日 起 有 效 期 為10年，除 非 另 行
  years from 7 July 2014, unless otherwise cancelled or        取消或修改。
  amended.

  The maximum number of shares currently permitted to          截至2018年8月31日，根據股份獎勵計
  be granted under the Share Award Scheme is limited           劃目前允許授予的最大股份數量限制為
  to 5% of the issued share capital of the Company as          本公司已發行股本的5%。可以授予股
  at 31 August 2018. The maximum number of shares              份獎勵計劃的每個合格參與者的最大股
  which may be awarded to each eligible participant in         份數量限於本集團於2018年8月31日已
  the Share Award Scheme is limited to 1% of the               發行股本的1%。
  issued share capital of the Group as at 31 August
  2018.

  The eligible participant for participation in the Share      參加股份獎勵計劃的合格參與 者（「獲
  Award Scheme (the “Selected Participant”) is selected        選參與者」）及根據股份獎勵計 劃 獎 勵
  and the number of shares to be awarded under the             的股份數量，由董事會選擇及決定。董
  Share Award Scheme is determined by the board of             事會還決定是否由獲選參與者支付價格
  directors. The board of directors also determines            來 授 予 股 份（「授 出 價 格」）和 授 出 價 格
  whether the shares will be awarded with or without           的 金 額。受 託 人（「受 託 人」）將 根 據 股

        Mainland China, a portion of the profits of the           本公司在中國內地的附屬公司的
        Company’s subsidiaries in Mainland China has              部分利潤已轉入法定盈餘公積，
        been transferred to the statutory surplus reserve         使用受限。

        the financial statements. The amount will either          股份獎勵歸屬時轉移至庫存股份
        be transferred to treasury share reserve and              儲備及資本公積，或在相關股份

        should the related share awards expire or be

      49. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                       49. 財務風險管理目標及政策
          OBJECTIVES AND POLICIES
           The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise               本集團的主要金融工具包括現金和現金
           cash and cash equivalents, restricted and pledged bank             等價物、限制性和已抵押銀行存款、其
           deposits, other financial assets at amortised cost,                他按攤銷成本計量的金融資產、以公允
           financial assets at FVTPL, payables to the Sponsored               價值計量且其變動計入損益的金融資
           Hospitals and interest-bearing bank and other                      產、應付舉辦權醫院款項以及計息銀行
           borrowings. The main purpose of these financial                    及其他借款。這些金融工具的主要目的
           instruments is to finance the Group’s operations. The              是為本集團的運營提供資金。本集團還
           Group has various other financial assets and liabilities           有其他各種金融資產和負債，例如來自
           such as receivables from IOT hospitals, loan to a                  IOT醫院之應收款項、向一間舉辦權醫
           sponsored hospital, trade and bills receivables, financial         院提供的貸款、應收貿易款項及應收票
           assets included in prepayments, deposits and other                 據、計入預付款項、保證金及其他應收
           receivables, balances with related parties, trade and              款項的金融資產、與關聯方的結餘、應
           bills payables, financial liabilities included in other            付貿易款項及應付票據、計入其他應付
           payables and accruals, and lease liabilities, which                款項及應計費用的金融負債，以及租賃
           mainly arise directly from its operations.                         負債，主要直接來自其經營活動。

           The main risks arising from the Group’s financial                  本集團金融工具產生的主要風險是利率
           instruments are interest rate risk, foreign currency risk,         風險、外匯風險、信貸風險、流動資金
           credit risk, liquidity risk and equity price risk. The board       風險及權益價格風險。董事會審查並同
           of directors reviews and agrees policies for managing              意管理每種風險的政策，現概述如下。
           each of these risks and they are summarised below.

           Interest rate risk                                                 利率風險
           The Group’s exposure to the risk of changes in market              本集團承受的市場利率變動風險主要與
           interest rates relates primarily to the Group’s loan to a          本集團向一間舉辦權醫院提供的貸款以
           sponsored hospital and interest-bearing bank                       及具有浮動利率的計息銀行借款有關。
           borrowings with floating interest rates. The Group does            本集團並無使用衍生金融工具對沖其利
           not use derivative financial instruments to hedge its              率風險。本集團通過密切監控利率變動
           interest rate risk. The Group mitigates this risk by               並定期審查其可用信貸額度及其使用情
           closely monitoring the movements in interest rates and             況來減輕這種風險。
           reviewing its available credit facilities and their
           utilisation regularly.

   Liquidity risk                                                               流動資金風險
   Liquidity risk is the risk that the Group will encounter                     流動資金風險指本集團因資金短缺而難
   difficulty in meeting financial obligations due to                           以履行財務責任的風險。本集團面臨的
   shortage of funds. The Group’s exposure to liquidity                         流動資金風險主要來自金融資產和負債
   risk arises primarily from mismatches of the maturities                      的到期日錯配。本集團通過考慮金融負
   of financial assets and liabilities. The Group monitors                      債和金融資產的到期日來監控資金短缺
   its risk to a shortage of funds by considering the                           的風險。
   maturities of both its financial liabilities and financial
   assets.

   The Group’s objective is to maintain a balance                               本集團的目標是通過使用銀行及其他借
   between continuity of funding and flexibility through                        款在資金的連續性和靈活性之間保持平
   the use of bank and other borrowings. The Group aims                         衡。本集團旨在維持足夠的現金及現金
   to maintain sufficient cash and cash equivalents to                          等價物，以滿足其流動資金需求。
   meet its liquidity requirements.
